(C4: the keep's breach is mended once the giant carries its ashlar - a change the masons stand beside; they still have no building animation) | the settlement package | `data/world/encounter_aldmere_warden_masons.json` |
| **Human playtest not run**: the telegraphs, the guard's readability and the camera at his size were judged on stills, the video and the bot. | the owner | - |
| **The Web world check's default walk failed 2 runs of 3 on a single music hole the main thread did not explain** (C1's tree passed twice on the same machine); the walk never comes near the keep. | the Web lane | `tools/web/world_check.mjs`, `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` ("C2") |

## From C3 (playability: the swings heard, the aim, the inventory, the forges and waystones, the motion)

What C3 left (`docs/systems/C3.md` "What is weak"; `docs/DECISIONS.md` §2.7 C3-1..C3-7).

| Item | Who | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **The sound was proven in headless Chromium only**: the effects now reach the output and their levels were measured there; no real browser, speaker or human ear has heard them. The mix's balance (an SFX bus at -6 dB, the music at -9) is the P19 plan's; a human may want the swings louder. | the owner (a playtest in his browser) | `data/audio/mix_default.json` (`bus_db`), `tools/web/sfx_check.mjs` |
| **Near voices have no panning**: a sound within its class's unit distance (the giant's own swings and steps, most of his blows' impacts) plays centred; at stage 8 that unit is 26-48 m, so a hit 20 m to his left plays centred. | an audio pass | `scripts/audio/audio_director.gd` (`play_request`), `scripts/audio/voice_pool.gd` |
| **A colossus cannot swing at a man or a fence before its feet** (stages 7 and 8: the indicator says "Out of reach"); its stomps and strides are what reach them. Whether the arcs should come down to the ground at those stages is a design question. | the owner / the next combat package | `data/growth/growth_07.json`, `growth_08.json` (`arc_pitch_offset_deg`), `scripts/actors/player/player_attack.gd` |
| **The aim indicator predicts standing targets**: a man walking during the windup can step out of the arc the brackets showed. | the next combat package | `scripts/actors/player/player_attack.gd` (`predicted_struck`) |
| **The third-person camera cannot look down more than 70 degrees**: at stages 7-8 a man within about 8 m of the feet cannot be put under the cursor (the soft target and the ground's pull still find him). | the camera's next pass | `scripts/presentation/camera_rig.gd` (`TP_PITCH_MIN`) |
| **The landmarks are one size**: a forge's smithy is built for a stage 1-3 giant (1.55 x for the titan-tier forges); at stages 7-8 it stands about his shin. The chimney's smoke, the fire's light and the place marker carry it from far, but a colossus does not work at it the way a small giant does. | a world art pass | `tools/builders/forge.gd` (`build_smithy`), `scripts/world/forge.gd` (`BIG_SMITHY_SCALE`) |
| **The looks have no solids of their own**: the old boxes' bodies stay (the stone's 1.6 m post, the hearth and the anvil), so the monument's plinth, the smithy's chimney above the hearth, its wall, trough and lean-to can be walked through (new solids blocked the Valley's stage-3 corridor by the bridge and are left for a rebuild that also bakes them into the navigation). | the world builder's next rebuild | `tools/builders/forge.gd`, `tools/world/nav_bake.gd`, `tools/builders/valley.gd` |
| **The looks' far reach**: the monument and the smithy draw to 240 m, the smoke to 420 m, a known waystone's beam to 2.4 km - set so the static world stays within 40 draw calls a view at low (great pines 40, the woodcutters' camp 39); an unreached waystone is not seen past 240 m. | a Web performance pass | `tools/builders/forge.gd`, `tests/gate/test_world_build.gd` |
| **The rig's blends were not changed**: the motion work is the motor's and the controller's (turns, the swing's turn into its blow, the attack buffer); no new clips, no foot-locking, no start/stop transition clips. | the giant's next motion pass | `scripts/presentation/skinned_rig.gd`, `tools/giant/kit/clips.py` |
| **The inventory's NEW marks are the session's**: a reload forgets what was seen (no save change). | the next save schema | `ui/screens/inventory_screen.gd` (`_seen`) |
| **Human playtest not run**: the five reports were checked by tests, bots, stills, films and the headless browser. | the owner | - |

## From C3b (the playability follow-up: what stands at the feet, the motion, the landmarks, the Web's holes, the near sounds)

What C3b left (`docs/systems/C3b.md` "What is weak"; `docs/DECISIONS.md` §2.7 C3b-1..C3b-7). C3's rows on the colossus's
reach, the camera's 70 degrees, the near voices' panning, the looks' solids and the rig's untouched blends are answered
there; the rest of "From C3" stands.

| Item | Who | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **The stamp is the pose brain's, not a clip**: the foot's lift and drive, the pelvis and the arms are keyed on the action's clock over the stance clip; it reads as a stamp at every stage but has no anticipation in the shoulders or the head's look down, and the club hangs where the arm's balance pose puts it. | the giant's next motion pass | `scripts/presentation/pose_brain_modifier.gd` (`_stamp_legs`), `scripts/presentation/actor_rig.gd` (`ability_pose` "foot_stamp"), `tools/giant/kit/clips.py` |
| **The low strike predicts a standing target**: its pulse strikes where the target stood when the press was planned (as the swing's indicator predicts standing targets); a running man can be out of the foot's ring when it lands. | the next combat package | `scripts/actors/player/player_attack.gd` (`stamp_point`, `_run_foot_stamp`) |
| ~~**A colossus's weapon is still carried past its fit**: at stages 7-8 the swings reach mid range with the stage-6 club, and tip matching draws it short of the tested tip (the HUD says it is too small; a stage-7 forge tier is C1's followup).~~ (C4: rimforged weapons fit stages 7-8, decision C4-9) | the forge tier package | `data/weapons/*.json` (`fit_max_stage`), `scripts/actors/player/player_equipment.gd` |
| **An unreached waystone's grey column draws 150-300 m only**: farther put three views of a new game over the static world's 40 draw calls at low (`test_world_build`); beyond it the HUD's far mark carries the stone. The landmarks are still one size (a smithy at a colossus's shin). | a Web performance pass (an instanced beam field would draw every column in one call) | `scripts/presentation/waystone_look.gd` (`UNLIT_FAR_M`), `tools/builders/forge.gd` |
| **The near voices' placement was measured, not heard**: on the Web the near voices start with their gains set and pan up to about 6 dB left or right (Godot's 3D panning at the project's strength); the giant's own steps sit near the middle. No real browser, speaker or ear has judged the balance. | the owner (a playtest) | `scripts/audio/voice_pool.gd`, `tools/web/sfx_check.mjs` |
| **The Web's music still breaks under software WebGL**: the check now places every hole of a starved page on the main thread (decision C3b-7); the plan's criterion (no hole over 50 ms) is met only where the frames run within the stream's buffer - a GPU machine's `--strict` run, still not run. | the owner (`docs/USABILITY_SCRIPT.md` §11) | `tools/web/world_check.mjs --strict` |
| **The hit-stop is the player's only**: a titan's or an ogre's blow on the giant does not hold; the bots never hold (their duels were tuned without it). | the next combat-feel pass | `scripts/actors/player/player_attack.gd` (`_hit_stop`), `scripts/combat/combat_controller.gd` (`hold_ticks`) |
| **Human playtest not run**: the low strike, the motion and the landmarks were checked by tests, films, stills and the headless browser. | the owner | - |


## From C4 (settlement development: Last Hearth's three tiers, the older settlements' second)

What C4 left (`docs/systems/C4.md` "What is weak"; `docs/DECISIONS.md` §2.7 C4-1..C4-12).

| Item | Who | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **The settlement is still low-poly primitives.** Dry-stone courses of seeded lumps, turf and thatch as flat slabs, hut roofs as cones, gate-stones as drums, banners as boards: it reads as a hold-outs' camp turned hold from a giant's height, not as a photographed place up close. No interior to the hall, no doors that open, no snow banked against walls. | a world art pass | `tools/world/kinds/settlement.gd` |
| **Work happens by a cut, not an animation**: a load set down or a work raised appears at once under dust, a shake and its sound; the giant has no lifting, setting or levering motion (the carried load uses the grab's two-handed hold pose), and the folk do not build - they stand where their encounters put them. | the giant's next motion pass; the people's next pass | `scripts/actors/player/player_haul.gd`, `scripts/world/settlement_site.gd` |
| **A dropped load goes back to its pile** (no load lies where the giant put it down, none survives a reload in his arms): honest, but a player who sets a king-stone down to fight walks back for it. | the settlement package's next pass | `PlayerHaul.put_back` |
| **The garrison's arrows cannot miss**: a post picks the nearest hostile in range and its hit lands when its streak arrives (no projectile, no cover); the archers stand on the firing steps at ground level and do not draw their bows. The raid's balance (seven soldiers and a rimkin against a stage-6 to 8 giant, the garrison's share) was measured by the gate walk's scripted kills only, not by a bot or a person. | the next combat package | `scripts/world/settlement_garrison.gd`, `data/world/encounter_tpl_hoarfell_hearth_raiders.json` |
| **The firelight is weak by day and the night is a capture's**: the hall's fire, the gate and the beacon carry flames, a doorway's glow and two lights that fade with distance; the game has no night, so the firelit stills use the tour's dusk. | a lighting pass (a day-night cycle is the owner's call) | `tools/world/kinds/settlement_fire.gd`, `tools/world_tour.gd` (`_dusk`) |
| **The world's data is at 4.94 of its 5.0 MB** after the settlements' meshes (compressed, preloaded); the next land's content needs the budget raised or the meshes cut. | the next world package | `tests/integration/test_world_data.gd` (`DATA_BUDGET`) |
| **The lead toward the Frostbound Titan is a placeholder**: Maera's watchers' sighting and a look from the Hoarwatch set `lead.frostbound.hearth`; milestone D consumes it. | milestone D | `data/quests/quest_hearth_frost_lead.json` |
| **The older settlements' second tier is one project each** and their first is their titan's aftermath; their folk and stalls are as act one left them. | a later settlement pass | `data/settlements/settlement_millford.json` and the others |
| **The settlement panel has no map pin for a need's place** (the words say where a load lies; the deeds name their markers in data only). | a UI pass | `ui/screens/settlement_screen.gd` |
| **Human playtest not run**: the hauling's pace (about six minutes of carrying for Last Hearth), the raid and the panel were checked by tests, the scripted walk, stills and the film. | the owner | - |

## From the milestone C gate

What the milestone C gate left (`docs/systems/Cgate.md`; `docs/DECISIONS.md` §3.11). C4's rows on the garrison's arrows
that cannot miss, the hauling's pace and the work by a cut are answered there in part; what remains of them is below.

| Item | Who | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **The ring is not solid**: the giant and the raiders walk through the curtain the giant raised (no solids: the folk's navmesh is the camp's at every tier), and a stage-6 giant is stopped by a garrison archer on his firing step. Solid flagged walls need a navmesh per tier; a giant passing through small friendly people is a motor rule for every friendly man. | a world package; the giant's next motion pass | `tools/world/kinds/settlement.gd`, `scripts/actors/player/player_motor.gd` |
| **The raid is tuned on one bot, three seeds**: the novice holds 9 of 9 over tiers 1-3 (its worst at 5 % health); idle at tiers 2-3 the raid ends in no outcome (the warleader alive, beyond the bows) rather than a loss; its first run comes as soon as the roof is raised. A person should fight it. | the owner (a playtest) | `data/world/event_hearth_raid.json`, `data/enemies/rimkin_warleader.json`, `scripts/world/settlement_garrison.gd` |
| **The camp's draw calls are the skinned folk** (about 50 of 97 at `low`; 121 on the Web with a raid): a camera-distance rule for skinning at `low` would cut them, for every crowd. | a Web performance pass | `scripts/presentation/person_rig.gd` (`_allocate`, `LOW_SKINNED`) |
| **Still low-poly up close**: the courses grey boxes, the roof flat turves, the domes smooth, the drifts white mounds; the Bastion keep's walls the Old Kingdom's pale stone boxes. The world's data is at 4.98 of 5.0 MB: more needs the budget raised or the meshes cut elsewhere. | a world art pass (the owner's budget call) | `tools/world/kinds/settlement.gd`, `tools/world/kinds/wall.gd`, `tests/integration/test_world_data.gd` |
| **Nothing is built by hand**: works rise out of the ground under dust; no lift, set-down or levering motion for the giant, the folk do not build. | the giant's next motion pass | `scripts/world/flag_visibility.gd`, `scripts/actors/player/player_haul.gd` |
| **Human playtest not run**: the raid, the haul's pace, the rise and the tower's fall were judged by bots, a scripted walk and software-rendered stills. | the owner | - |

## From D3 (stage 9, the Elder Colossus; the growth track's requirements made real)

What D3 left (`docs/systems/D3.md` "What is weak"; `docs/DECISIONS.md` §2.7 D3-1..D3-12).

| Item | Who | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **The XP curve overshoots**: on the guaranteed path the Frostbound's kill (3,600 XP) takes a giant from level 15 to 19, and the Stormcaller's fall to the cap (20); milestones E-F's levels 18-20 have no XP left to give. D3 only floors level 17 at the Stormcaller's fall (no grind). A rebalance (the titans' XP, the curve past 15, or both) is milestone F's ("balance to level 20"). | milestone F | `data/enemies/frostbound.json`, `data/enemies/stormcaller.json` (`xp_reward`), `data/progression/progression_default.json` |

A great rim-fall heaved by a stage-9 permission, or a place only a 116 ft giant reaches, was not built. | a world package | `tools/data/world/hoarfell.json`, `data/growth/growth_09.json` |
